## `AFG-3510-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Stator-Cooling-Conductivity Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Power Quality Analysis
- **Intro:** Stator-Cooling-Conductivity Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Power Quality Analysis — Pre-calibration safety and system readiness verification: Ambient temperature: 20°C to 30°C; relative humidity: 30% to 70% RH; reference standard uncertainty: ≤ 0.5% of reading.
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 20°C to 30°C; relative humidity: 30% to 70% RH; reference standard uncertainty: ≤ 0.5% of reading.
- **Standards:** `IEC 60034-1:2022`, `IEC 60034-2-1:2014`, `IEC 60214-1:2014`, `IEEE 115-2019`

## `AGO-3510-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Boiler-Turbine-Generator Unit Leader
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Power Plant Grid-Connected Generator Central Control Room
- **Intro:** Boiler-Turbine-Generator Unit Leader — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Power Plant Grid-Connected Generator Central Control Room — Verify Permit-to-Work and LOTO Status: All LOTO tags must be in place; no voltage above 50 V AC or 120 V DC on any bus; grounding switch closed with visible ground.
